# blueprint · pages as read

## B001 · Blueprint · A General structure · 七杀格 · 丙 Underfueled · born 2006-12-3 10:00

**1 · STRUCTURE** A General structure · 七杀格 [STRUCTURE qisha_ge · reading]
Your chart answers to pressure, the hard test and the person who pushes back. You rise when the stakes are real and the deadline is close, and you are steadiest in the thick of a fight.

**1 · WHY** [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · structure_rule.none]
Nothing hidden in your strongest seat shows on your stems. The General who holds it is the organising force of your life, and you answer to it first.

**1 · STATE** Formed, under strain. [STRUCTURE officer · status.strained.mixed]
The Magistrate shows beside your General, and you switch between fighting for ground and asking for it politely. People above you get mixed signals, and you lose credit that one clear approach would win.

**1 · WHAT PROTECTS IT** what protects it · Artisan decade 2087 to 2096 [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · rescue.output]
What you make protects it, work you finish and show, and you find the time to make it in your decades later.

**2 · STRONGEST** [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · strongest.two]
Water and Wood carry three quarters of your chart, and you live mostly through what you answer to and what you think.

**2 · MISSING** no Sage · it arrives with your decade 2057 to 2066 [GOD zhengyin · missing]
Your chart holds no Sage, and nobody inside it shelters you or praises you for trying. You learned early to reassure yourself, and you trust help more when you did not have to ask for it.

**2 · SEASON** 十月丙 [SEASON bing.hai · reading]
Early winter takes the sun out of season, with short days and cold water rising under you. The month wants Wood to keep your fire fed, Earth to hold back the water, and Metal to trim the Wood when it grows thick. You stay bright through the dark months by drawing on each of these as the weather changes.

**2 · SEASON NEED** first need · 甲, the Alchemist · inside a branch, not on show [GOD pianyin · season_need.rooted]
The Alchemist your season asks for runs underneath without showing, and you study in private and keep the strangest of what you learn to yourself.

**2 · SEASON NEED** second need · 戊, the Artisan · inside a branch, not on show [GOD shishen · season_need.rooted]
The Artisan your season asks for stays beneath the surface, and you refill yourself through a private craft or a kitchen nobody else uses.

**2 · SEASON NEED** second need · 庚, the Horizon · hidden deep [GOD piancai · season_need.hidden]
Your season asks for the Horizon and your chart keeps only a thin seam of it, and you stumble on your chances late, through a friend of a friend.

**2 · SEASON NEED** support · 壬, the General · inside a branch, not on show [GOD qisha · season_need.rooted]
The General your season asks for works unseen from within, and you hold yourself to a hard private standard that makes you stronger.

**3 · IN PLAIN VIEW** the Twin [GOD bijian · seen.plain]
You come across as someone who carries their own weight and asks for little help.

**3 · IN PLAIN VIEW** the Virtuoso [GOD shangguan · seen.plain]
You come across as quick and outspoken, the one who says what the rest of the meeting was thinking.

**3 · IN PLAIN VIEW** the Magistrate [GOD zhengguan · seen.plain]
You come across as reliable and correct, the person others trust to follow the rules and keep their word.

**3 · INSIDE** the Artisan [GOD shishen · seen.inside]
You want comfort more than you show, good food and an evening with nothing planned.

**3 · INSIDE** the General [GOD qisha · seen.inside]
You push yourself harder in private than anyone asks of you, and you set tests that nobody else sees.

**3 · INSIDE** the Alchemist [GOD pianyin · seen.inside]
You think in strange directions and keep most of those thoughts to yourself.

**3 · INSIDE** the Twin [GOD bijian · seen.inside]
In private you want to answer to nobody, and you would rather finish a job alone than wait for a hand.

**3 · CLOSE** [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · seen.close]
Only the people you live with meet the second list.

**4 · PAIRING** The Artisan tames the General · 食神制杀 [TG_PATTERN shishen_zhi_sha · blueprint_line]
A hard deadline settles you instead of rattling you, and you turn out your best work when the stakes are high and the clock runs short.

**4 · PAIRING** The Alchemist starves the Artisan · 枭神夺食 [TG_PATTERN xiao_shen_duo_shi · blueprint_line]
Your thinking outweighs your making, and you study a thing so long that the moment to make it passes.

**4 · PAIRING** The Virtuoso confronts the Magistrate · 伤官见官 [TG_PATTERN shang_guan_jian_guan · blueprint_line]
Your talent argues with whoever is in charge, and you are at your sharpest where nobody signs off on every line.

**4 · BOND** Day binds with Month · 寅亥合 [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · bond.bind.month_day]
You find a partner close to your work or your parents' circle, and the line between your job and your home stays thin for years.

**4 · BOND** Hour clashes with Month · 巳亥冲 [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · bond.clash.month_hour]
What you built at work in your rising years stops fitting your later life, and you change course in your late forties rather than hold on to it.

**4 · BOND** Hour harms Day · 寅巳害 [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · bond.harm.day_hour]
A quiet friction runs between your partner and the people after you, and you spend years smoothing over complaints nobody says out loud.

**4 · BOND** Hour punishes Day · 寅巳刑 [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · bond.punish.day_hour]
The same argument returns at home between you and the people after you, and you break the loop by changing your own rule first.

## B003 · Blueprint · A Virtuoso structure · 伤官格 · 丙 Underfueled · born 2004-1-8 1:00

**1 · STRUCTURE** A Virtuoso structure · 伤官格 [STRUCTURE shangguan_ge · reading]
Your chart turns on a sharp voice and a talent that refuses to sit quietly. You shine where you are judged on what you can do, on a stage or under your own name, and you do your finest work when nobody stands over you.

**1 · WHY** [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · structure_rule.main]
The Virtuoso who holds your strongest seat also shows on one of your stems. That makes it the organising force of your life, and you answer to it before anything else.

**1 · STATE** Formed. [STRUCTURE output · status.formed.none]
What you make and say reaches people without getting stuck on the way. Your talent opens doors for you, and the work you enjoy keeps finding someone who wants it.

**2 · STRONGEST** [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · strongest.one]
Earth alone carries two thirds of your chart, and you live mostly through what you make.

**2 · MISSING** no Twin · no decade of yours brings it [GOD bijian · missing]
Your chart has no Twin, and nobody inside it stands level with you or carries half the load. You find your footing through other people, a team or a close friend, and you stand firmest when someone stands beside you.

**2 · MISSING** no Horizon · it arrives with your decade 2055 to 2064 [GOD piancai · missing]
With no Horizon in your chart, nobody inside it opens a side door or brings you an offer you did not chase. You earn by the front route, one job at a time, and you trust what you worked for over a windfall.

**2 · MISSING** no General · your season asks for it too · it arrives with your decade 2035 to 2044 [GOD qisha · missing]
Your chart is without the General, and nobody inside it sets you a hard test or a date you cannot move. You keep your own pace and face few real tests, and you learn how strong you are only when pressure comes from outside.

**2 · MISSING** no Alchemist · your season asks for it too · your Alchemist decade 2015 to 2024 is lived [GOD pianyin · missing]
Nobody inside your chart pulls you toward odd subjects or second thoughts, because it holds no Alchemist. You take knowledge as it is taught, and you act on a straight answer before you think to question it.

**2 · SEASON** 十二月丙 [SEASON bing.chou · reading]
Late winter gives your sun its nerve back, strong enough to laugh at the snow and melt the frost. This month looks to Water to carry that new strength, and with so much frozen Earth about it calls on Wood to break the ground. You outshine the last of the cold once the soil loosens.

**3 · IN PLAIN VIEW** the Magistrate [GOD zhengguan · seen.plain]
You come across as reliable and correct, the person others trust to follow the rules and keep their word.

**3 · IN PLAIN VIEW** the Sage [GOD zhengyin · seen.plain]
You come across as calm and well taught, someone a teacher or an older friend has clearly looked after.

**3 · IN PLAIN VIEW** the Virtuoso [GOD shangguan · seen.plain]
You come across as quick and outspoken, the one who says what the rest of the meeting was thinking.

**3 · INSIDE** the Virtuoso [GOD shangguan · seen.inside]
You rewrite other people's work in your head, and you want your talent noticed more than you admit.

**3 · INSIDE** the Artisan [GOD shishen · seen.inside]
You want comfort more than you show, good food and an evening with nothing planned.

**3 · CLOSE** [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · seen.close]
Only the people you live with meet the second list.

**4 · PAIRING** The Virtuoso harnessed by the Sage · 伤官配印 [TG_PATTERN shang_guan_pei_yin · blueprint_line]
Your sharpest talent has years of study behind it, and you are known for being both daring and right.

**4 · PAIRING** The Virtuoso confronts the Magistrate · 伤官见官 [TG_PATTERN shang_guan_jian_guan · blueprint_line]
Your talent argues with whoever is in charge, and you are at your sharpest where nobody signs off on every line.

**4 · BOND** Month clashes with Year · 丑未冲 [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · bond.clash.year_month]
Your choice of work breaks with what your family expected, and you leave home early to make your own way in a trade nobody there chose.

**4 · BOND** Month punishes Year · 丑未刑 [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · bond.punish.year_month]

**4 · BOND** Day punishes Year · 戌未刑 [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · bond.punish.year_day]

**4 · BOND** Hour punishes Year · 丑未刑 [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · bond.punish.year_hour]

**4 · BOND** Day punishes Month · 丑戌刑 [TEMPLATED tpl_depth · bond.punish.month_day]

**2 · MISSING** no Alchemist · no decade of yours brings it [GOD pianyin · missing]

Late autumn walls you in with Earth, and your flow slows behind the dam. Wood comes first to work that Earth loose and give you an outlet, and the warmth of the sun's Fire follows to stir what has gone still. You move again once both are present, and the stillness turns into purpose.

**3 · IN PLAIN VIEW** the Steward [GOD zhengcai · seen.plain]

Your chart takes its shape from your own strength, with little handed down and most things built by hand. You do best where effort is paid to you directly, and you are proudest of what you made with nobody's help.
